```
def softmax(x):
    y=x-np.max(x); e=np.exp(y); return e/e.sum()
```

def lse_p_score_grad(p, beta): # p normalized
   n=len(p); q=fast_conv(p); cmax=q.max(); scaled=beta*(q-cmax); exps=np.exp(scaled); v=exps/exps.sum(); L=cmax + np.log(exps.sum())/beta; score=2*n*L
   revp=p[::-1]
   r=fast_conv_len(revp, v, length=2*n-1)??? We can use fft_conv(a,b): full length len(a)+len(b)-1.
   r=conv_2(revp, v)[n-1:2*n-1]
   vq=np.dot(v,q)
   grad=2.0*p*(r-vq)
   return score, grad
```

Gradient computation details for x log:
```
def lse_x_obj(x, beta):
    p = softmax(x)
    q = fast_conv(p)
    cmax = q.max()
    scaled = beta*(q-cmax)
    exps = np.exp(scaled)
    zsum = exps.sum()
    L = cmax + np.log(zsum)/beta
    score = 2*len(p)*L
    v = exps/zsum
    vq = np.dot(v,q)
    r = conv(revp, v)[n-1:2n-1]
    grad = 2.0*p*(r-vq)
    return score, grad
```
But `minimize` expects function returning (f,g) if jac=True, not tuple? For method L-BFGS-B jac=True can pass fun returning both? In scipy, if jac=True, fun should return tuple (f,g) since SciPy? Usually fun can return (f,g) and jac True. Yes. Or define separate fun returning f and grad returning g, but no closures? We can pass args to fun and a grad function? Easier: use `wandb?` No. We can define `lse_x_value(x,beta)` and `lse_x_grad(x,beta)` and use minimize with fun and jac? But that doubles FFT. Instead use tuple.

Scipy minimize with jac=True and fun returning tuple: According to docs, if jac is True, fun should return [f, g]. Works for L-BFGS-B? I think yes. But to be safe, we can set `jac=lse_x_obj`? No. We can pass `fun=lse_x_value` and `jac=lse_x_grad` but recomputes. For performance, tuple is okay in current SciPy. We'll use tuple.

Need `fast_conv_general(a,b)` for arbitrary lengths:
```
def conv_general(a,b):
    M=len(a)+len(b)-1
    N=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); B=np.fft.rfft(b,N); return np.fft.irfft(A*B,N)[:M]
```
For self conv p: use conv_general(p,p). For speed, if a is b, can square. But okay.

Upsampling/interpolation:
```
def resample_p(p, n_new):
  n=len(p); if n==n_new return p
  x_old=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
  x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)/n_new
  y=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
  y=np.clip(y,0,None)
  s=y.sum(); if s<=0: y=np.ones(n_new)
  return y/s
```
This might over-smooth; okay.

def random_smooth_x(rng,n, band):

Detailed functions:

1. `normalize_raw(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum(); if s<=1e-12: return np.ones(64)*0.5; return a/s
```

2. `score_p(p)`: assumes p nonnegative maybe not normalized:
```
n=len(p); S=p.sum(); if S<1e-8: return inf; q=fft_conv_self(p); return 2*n*q.max()/(S*S)
```
If p normalized sum1, S=1.

3. `fft_conv_self(a)`:
Use next fast length for 2n-1. Since repeated, M=2*n-1; N=1 << (M-1).bit_length(); `A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); res=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]`. For general conv: `fft_conv(a,b)`.

4. `softmax_vec(x)`:
```
x=x-np.max(x); e=np.exp(x); s=e.sum(); return e/s
```

5. `logp_to_p(x)`: softmax.

6. `lse_x_score_grad(x, beta)`:
```
n=len(x); p=softmax_vec(x); q=fft_conv_self(p); c=q.max(); scaled=beta*(q-c); e=np.exp(scaled); z=e.sum(); L=c + np.log(z)/beta; score=2*n*L
v=e/z; vq=np.dot(v,q)
r=fft_conv(p[::-1], v); # length 3n-2
r=r[n-1:2*n-1]
grad=2.0*p*(r-vq)
return score, grad
```
Potential issue `fft_conv(p[::-1], v)` length n+(2n-1)-1=3n-2. Okay. For n large, memory.
Can we avoid second FFT by using q and v? Maybe r = cross-corr; convolution needed.

7. `run_lbfgs_from_p(p, beta, maxiter, maxfun)`:
```
x=np.log(p+1e-300)
res=minimize(lse_x_score_grad, x, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':30})
x=res.x; p=softmax_vec(x); return score_p(p), p
```
If res.fun is inf? fallback p.

Need beta parameter: For run, choose beta. `beta_list_for_p(p, gammas)` compute qmax:
```
q=fft_conv_self(p); qm=q.max(); if qm<=0: qm=1e-12; return [g/qm for g in gammas]
```
But qm for p sum1 ~1/n. gamma [1,3,10,30,100] yields beta small to large. Use maxfun decreasing.

8. `upsample_p(p, n_new)`: as above.
9. `downsample_p(p, n_new)`: if n_new>=len maybe no; use interp average? Use `np.interp` similar for downsample (linear). Or pool by blocks.
```
def resample_p(p, n_new):
    n=len(p)
    if n==n_new: return p.copy()
    xo=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n; xn=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)/n_new
    y=np.interp(xn, xo, p)
    y=np.clip(y,0,None); s=y.sum(); if s<=0: y=np.ones(n_new)
    return y/s
```
Works for up/down.

10. `random_smooth_x(rng, n, band)`:
```
z=rng.standard_normal(n)
F=np.fft.rfft(z)
freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
keep=np.exp(-(5*band*freq)**2)??? freq normalized cycles/sample. For low-pass, keep = np.exp(- (freq*band)**2)
F*=keep
x=np.fft.irfft(F,n) + rng.normal(0,0.2,n)
return x
```
`band` ~5-20.

11. `param_x(rng, n)`:
Generate p shape then log:
- Choose type 0: p = 1 + a*x + b*(x*x -1/3) + c*cos(2π r x+φ); clip min eps; x=log(p)
- type 1: mixture of up to 3 Gaussians positive.
```
def simple_param_x(rng,n):
    x=np.arange(n)/n
    typ=rng.integers(0,4)
    if typ==0: p = 1 + rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)*x
    elif typ==1: p = (1-x)**a + eps
    elif typ==2: p = sum gaussians
    p=clip(p,1e-6,None); return np.log(p)
```
Not crucial.

12. `cd_hot_sweep(p, rng, hot_frac, iters, n_active, time_limit?)`:
```
def cd_hot_sweep(p, rng, hot_count=None, iters=12, active_count=32):
    a=p.astype(float).copy(); n=len(a); q=fft_conv_self(a); S=a.sum()
    if hot_count is None: hot_count=max(200, n//5)
    # hot indices (if rng not None random mix)
    # active lags
    M=2*n-1
    # active_count lags: top active_count by q
    act_idx=np.argpartition(-q, active_count-1)[:active_count]
    hot=np.zeros(n)
    for k in act_idx:
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(k,n-1)
       # others lo..hi other indices k-lo down to k-hi
       # weights p[j]*p[k-j]
       w=a[lo:hi+1] * a[k-lo:k-hi-1:-1]  # check endpoints
       hot[lo:hi+1] += w
    # random fraction
    top_k=int(0.8*hot_count)
    if top_k>0:
       top=np.argpartition(-hot, top_k)[:top_k]
       other=np.setdiff1d(np.arange(n), top, assume_unique=False)
       rand_count=hot_count-top_k
       if rand_count>0 and len(other)>rand_count:
          rand=rng.choice(other, size=rand_count, replace=False)
       else: rand=[]
       idx=np.concatenate([top, rand])
    else: idx=np.array([], dtype=int)
    if rng is not None: idx=rng.permutation(idx)
    # order maybe sort by hot? random.
    for i in idx:
       i=int(i)
       x=a[i]; S_rest=S-x
       k_lo=i; k_hi=i+n-1  # within M-1
       # outside
       if k_lo==0: om_left=0.0
       else: om_left=float(np.max(a[:k_lo]))
       if k_hi==M-1: om_right=0.0
       else: om_right=float(np.max(a[k_hi+1:]))
       om=max(om_left, om_right, 0.0)
       # base, alpha, beta
       base=a[i:i+n].copy()  # q slice
       old=2.0*x*a
       old[i]=x*x
       base -= old
       alpha=2.0*a.copy()
       alpha[i]=0.0
       beta=np.zeros(n); beta[i]=1.0
       # line search domain
       t_upper = max(5.0*abs(x)+1e-12, 100.0/n)
       if t_upper > max(1.0, 10.0/S if S>0 else 1.0): t_upper=1.0
       # But if x large? S around1. t_upper<=1.
       res=goldensearch_coord(0.0, t_upper, base, alpha, beta, om, S_rest, n, iters)
       t_opt=res
       if t_opt <0: t_opt=0
       a[i]=t_opt; S=S_rest+t_opt
       a[i:i+n] = base + t_opt*alpha + (t_opt*t_opt)*beta
    # normalize
    Sfinal=a.sum()
    if Sfinal>1e-12: a=a/Sfinal
    return a, score_p(a)
```

def polish_sequence(p, rng, budget_deadline):
    # p input best at start
    p_cur=p; sc=score_p(p_cur); best_local=p_cur; best_score_local=sc
    # beta chain
    qmax=score/(2n)
    gammas=[1,3,10,30]
    maxfuns=[4000,3000,2000,1500]
    for g,mf in zip:
       if time>deadline-... break
       beta=g / max(qmax,1e-12)
       sc,p2=run_lbfgs(p_cur,beta, maxiter=2000,maxfun=mf)
       if sc < best_local: update p_cur=p2
       # cd hot 1-2 sweeps if time
       p3,sc3=cd_hot_sweep(p_cur, rng, hot_count=max(500,n//5), iters=10)
       if sc3<best: p_cur=p3
    # perturb cycles
    while time < deadline_local_budget:
       p_pert = perturb_p(p_cur, rng, strategy)
       # quick lbfgs high beta or cd hot 2 sweeps
       ...
    return best_local
```

 # maybe param random at top lengths few

    p_init=softmax(x_init)
    # maybe evaluate p_init

    p2=sc_2=run_lbfgs(p1, beta=5/qmax, maxfun=600)

Define:
```
def lse_x_value(x,beta):
    sc, _ = lse_x_tuple(x,beta); return sc
def lse_x_grad(x,beta):
    _, g = lse_x_tuple(x,beta); return g
```
In run:
```
try:
    res=minimize(lse_x_tuple, x0, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=opts)
except Exception:
    res=minimize(lse_x_value, x0, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=lse_x_grad, options=opts)
```
The fallback `jac=lse_x_grad` expects function gfun(x,*args), okay. It will recompute. Good.

In `coord_1d_obj`, make sure `center` is an int offset. It modifies `arr` to set center. If center can be >= len base? For affected length n, offset i within 0..n-1, yes.
```
def coord_1d_obj(t, base, alpha, center, outside, S_rest, n):
    S = S_rest + t
    if S <= 1e-300: return 1e100
    if center < 0:
        m=float(np.max(base + t*alpha))
    else:
        arr = base + t*alpha
        arr[center] = base[center] + t*t
        m=float(np.max(arr))
    tot = m if m > outside else outside
    return 2.0 * n * tot / (S*S)
```
If n small and t large, S*S okay.

`goldensearch_coord`:

def goldensearch_coord(lo, hi, base, alpha, center, outside, S_rest, n, iters):
    if hi <= lo: return lo
    gr = 0.6180339887498949
    best_t=lo; best_f=coord_1d_obj(lo,...)
    fhi=coord_1d_obj(hi,...); if fhi<best: update
    c=hi-gr*(hi-lo); d=lo+gr*(hi-lo)
    fc=...; fd=...
    for _ in range(iters):
       if fc < fd:
          hi=d; d=c; fd=fc; c=hi-gr*(hi-lo); fc=coord(c); update
       else:
          lo=c; c=d; fc=fd; d=lo+gr*(hi-lo); fd=coord(d); update
    mid=0.5*(lo+hi); fm=coord(mid); update
    return best_t
```
To reduce evals, initial fhi maybe not needed; but include. Number ~14.

`polish_sequence` top-level:
```
def polish_sequence(p, rng, deadline):
    p = np.asarray(p, float); p=np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum(); p = p/s if s>0 else ...
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score_p(p)
    n=len(p)
    # if n too small? no.
    # Beta estimate
    qmax = best_score/(2.0*n) if best_score<inf else 1.0/n
    if qmax<=0: qmax=1.0/n
    # maxfun based on n
    if n <= 1024: mfs=[3000,2000,1500,1000]
    elif n <= 4096: mfs=[2200,1600,1200,800]
    elif n <= 12000: mfs=[1600,1200,900,600]
    else: mfs=[1200,900,600,500]
    gammas=[1.0,3.0,10.0,30.0]
    p_cur=p
    for idx,(g,mf) in enumerate(zip(gammas,mfs)):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30: break
       beta = g / qmax
       sc, p2 = run_lbfgs_from_p(p_cur, beta, maxiter=mf, maxfun=mf) # maxiter same
       if sc < best_score - 1e-12: best... p_cur=p2
       elif sc < best_score + 1e-9: p_cur=p2? Maybe if not worse, use p2.
       # cd hot
       hc=choose_hot_count(n); it=choose_cd_iters(n); ac=choose_active_count(n)
       p3, sc3 = cd_hot_sweep(p_cur, rng, hc, it, ac)
       if sc3 < best_score: update p_cur=p3
    # if n large and time left, maybe additional CD sweeps
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 40:
       hc=...; p2,sc2=cd_hot_sweep(p_cur...)
       if sc2 < best_score: p_cur=p2
       else: break? Or perturb.
    # perturb cycles
    improve_no=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 45:
       strat=int(rng.integers(0,4))
       p_pert = perturb_p(p_cur,rng,strat)
       # quick lbfgs high beta small maxfun
       qmax_cur=score_p? best_score/(2n)
       beta = 20.0 / (best_score/(2.0*n) if ... else 1.0/n)
       sc1,p1=run_lbfgs_from_p(p_pert,beta,maxiter=500,maxfun=500)
       p2,sc2=cd_hot_sweep(p1,rng, choose_hot_count(n), choose_cd_iters(n), choose_active_count(n))
       if min(sc1,sc2) < best_score: update etc; improve_no=0
       else: improve_no+=1
       if improve_no>=10: break
    return best_p,best_score
```

def polish_sequence(p, rng, deadline):
    p = ...; best_p=p; best_score=score_p(p)
    n=len(p)
    p_cur=p
    qmax = best_score/(2.0*n) if best_score < 1e300 else 1.0/n
    if qmax <= 0: qmax=1.0/n
    # beta gammas and maxfuns as list
    ...
    for g,mf in ...:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 40: break
       beta = g / qmax
       sc,p2 = run_lbfgs_from_p(p_cur, beta, mf, mf)
       if sc < best_score + 1e-12:
           if sc < best_score: best... print
           p_cur = p2 if sc < best_old? maybe even if not improve? Use p_cur=p2 if finite.
       # cd
       if time...:
          sc3,p3=cd_hot_sweep(...)
          if sc3 < best_score: update; p_cur=p3
          elif time...
    # extra CD until no improvement or time
    stale=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 60:
       sc,p2=cd_hot_sweep(p_cur,...)
       if sc < best_score - 1e-12: update; p_cur=p2; stale=0
       else:
          # perturb
          strat=int(rng.integers(0,3))
          pp=perturb_p(p_cur,rng,strat)
          # low quick lbfgs high beta
          sc1,p1=run_lbfgs_from_p(pp, 20.0/qmax, 500,500)
          sc2,p2=cd_hot_sweep(p1,...)
          if min(sc1,sc2) < best_score: update; p_cur=...; stale=0
          else:
             stale+=1
             if stale>=5: break
    return best_p,best_score

```
def perturb_p(p, rng, strategy):
   n=len(p); out=p.copy()
   if strategy==0:
       x=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)) + 0.2*rng.standard_normal(n)
       return softmax_vec(x)
   if strategy==1:
       # random block zero/redistribute
       blen=max(8,n//80)
       start=int(rng.integers(0,n-blen+1))
       saved=out[start:start+blen].sum()
       out[start:start+blen]=0
       mask=np.ones(n,bool); mask[start:start+blen]=False
       cnt=int(mask.sum())
       if cnt>0: out[mask]+=saved/cnt
       s=out.sum(); return out/s if s>0 else p
   if strategy==2:
       # swap segments
       slen=max(8,n//40)
       s1=int(rng.integers(0,n-slen)); s2=int(rng.integers(0,n-slen))
       if s1==s2: return p
       tmp=out[s1:s1+slen].copy(); out[s1:s1+slen]=out[s2:s2+slen]; out[s2:s2+slen]=tmp
       s=out.sum(); return out/s if s>0 else p
   if strategy==3:
       # smooth noise
       x=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); x += 0.1*rng.standard_normal(n); x += 0.1*np.roll(x,2)-0.1*np.roll(x,-2)? no.
       return softmax_vec(x)
   return p
```
Block zero can create zeros; log later eps.

`random_smooth_x`:
```
def random_smooth_x(rng, n, band):
    z=rng.standard_normal(n)
    F=np.fft.rfft(z)
    freq=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    F=np.exp(-(freq*band)**2) * F
    x=np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    x = x / (np.std(x)+1e-12)
    return x + 0.1*rng.standard_normal(n)
```
Since softmax invariant to scale, okay.

def param_x(rng, n):
   x=np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1)

   elif typ==2: centers 1-3 gaussians

```
def run_lbfgs_from_p(p, beta, maxiter, maxfun):
    n=len(p)
    p0=np.clip(p,0,None).astype(float); s=p0.sum(); if s<=0: p0=np.ones(n); s=1
    p0=p0/s
    x=np.log(np.maximum(p0,1e-300))
    x=x-x.max()
    opts={'ftol':1e-13,'gtol':1e-10,'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':30}
    try:
       res = minimize(lse_x_tuple, x, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=opts)
    except Exception:
       res = minimize(lse_x_value, x, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=lse_x_grad, options=opts)
    if isinstance(res.x, np.ndarray): xout=res.x else xout=x
    pup=(softmax_vec(xout)); s=pup.sum(); if s>0 pup=pup/s
    sc=score_p(pup); if not finite sc: return score_p(p0), p0
    return sc,pup
```
If `minimize` returns res.x but maybe `res` is OptimizeResult. Good.

`fft_conv(a,b)`:
```
def fft_conv(a,b):
    M=len(a)+len(b)-1
    if M <=0: return np.array([])
    N=1 << (M-1).bit_length()
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); B=np.fft.rfft(b,N)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*B,N)[:M]
```
For self, can use same generic, but `fft_conv_self` squares:
```
def fft_conv_self(a):
   n=len(a); M=2*n-1; N=1 << (M-1).bit_length(); A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); return np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:M]
```
If array real. Good.

`score_p`:

S=np.sum(p); if S<=0 return inf; q=fft_conv_self(p); return float(2*n*np.max(q)/(S*S))
```

def param_x(rng,n):
    x=np.arange(n)/n
    typ=rng.integers(0,6)
    if typ==0:
       a=rng.uniform(-0.8,0.8); p=1+a*(2*x-1)
    elif typ==1:
       a=rng.uniform(-0.8,0.8); p=1+a*(6*x*x-6*x+1)
    elif typ==2:
       # two gaussian bumps
       p=np.zeros(n)
       for _ in range(int(rng.integers(1,4))):
          c=rng.uniform(0,1); w=rng.uniform(0.05,0.3); amp=rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)
          p+=amp*np.exp(-((x-c)/w)**2)
       p+=0.05
    elif typ==3:
       p=1
       for m in range(1,4):
          if rng.random()<0.5: p+= rng.uniform(-0.3,0.3)*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x+ rng.uniform(0,2π))
       p+=0.2
    else:
       p=np.exp(np.interp(x, [0,0.1,0.5,0.9,1], list of random positive))
    return log(clip(p,1e-8))
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / (sum(a))^2.

The program uses:
  - a parent sequence (height_sequence_1) if available,
  - multiresolution resampling,
  - coarse global optimization in log-softmax space with LSE-smoothed scores,
  - large-n refinement via L-BFGS-B on the same smoothed objective,
  - fast coordinate-descent updates on the exact nonsmooth objective,
  - perturbation / hill-climb cycles.
"""

import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time


def normalize_nonneg_raw(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
    return a / s


def fft_conv(a, b):
    M = len(a) + len(b) - 1
    if M <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    N = 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * B, N)[:M]


def fft_conv_self(a):
    n = len(a)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1 << (M - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]


def softmax_vec(x):
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(len(x), dtype=np.float64) / len(x)
    return e / s


def score_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(p)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(p.sum())
    if S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    q = fft_conv_self(p)
    return float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(q)) / (S * S))


def lse_x_tuple(x, beta):
    n = len(x)
    p = softmax_vec(x)
    q = fft_conv_self(p)
    cmax = float(np.max(q))
    scaled = beta * (q - cmax)
    expv = np.exp(scaled)
    zsum = float(expv.sum())
    if zsum <= 0.0:
        zsum = 1e-300
    L = cmax + float(np.log(zsum)) / beta
    score = float(2.0 * n * L)
    v = expv / zsum
    vq = float(np.dot(v, q))
    r = fft_conv(p[::-1], v)
    r = r[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    grad = 2.0 * p * (r - vq)
    return score, grad


def lse_x_value(x, beta):
    f, _ = lse_x_tuple(x, beta)
    return f


def lse_x_grad(x, beta):
    _, g = lse_x_tuple(x, beta)
    return g


def run_lbfgs_from_p(p, beta, maxiter, maxfun):
    p0 = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p0 = np.clip(p0, 0.0, None)
    s = float(p0.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        p0 = np.ones(len(p0), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(p0.sum())
    p0 = p0 / s

    x = np.log(np.maximum(p0, 1e-300))
    x = x - float(np.max(x))

    opts = {
        "maxiter": int(maxiter),
        "maxfun": int(maxfun),
        "ftol": 1e-13,
        "gtol": 1e-10,
        "maxls": 20,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(lse_x_tuple, x, args=(beta,), method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True, options=opts)
        xout = getattr(res, "x", x)
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = minimize(lse_x_value, x, args=(beta,), method="L-BFGS-B",
                           jac=lse_x_grad, options=opts)
            xout = getattr(res, "x", x)
        except Exception:
            xout = x

    if not isinstance(xout, np.ndarray):
        xout = x
    p_out = softmax_vec(xout)
    s = float(p_out.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        p_out = p0
    else:
        p_out = p_out / s
    sc = score_p(p_out)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return score_p(p0), p0
    return sc, p_out


def resample_p(p, n_new):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(p)
    n_new = int(max(1, n_new))
    if n == n_new:
        return p.copy()
    xo = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / n
    xn = (np.arange(n_new, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / n_new
    y = np.interp(xn, xo, p)
    y = np.clip(y, 0.0, None)
    s = float(y.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        y = np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n_new)
    return y / s


def random_smooth_x(rng, n, band):
    z = rng.standard_normal(n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(z)
    freq = np.fft.rfftfreq(n)
    F = np.exp(-((freq * band) ** 2)) * F
    x = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    sd = float(np.std(x))
    if sd > 1e-12:
        x = x / sd
    x = x + 0.1 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    return x


def param_x(rng, n):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n - 1)
    typ = int(rng.integers(0, 6))
    if typ == 0:
        a = rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85)
        p = 1.0 + a * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
    elif typ == 1:
        a = rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85)
        p = 1.0 + a * (6.0 * x * x - 6.0 * x + 1.0)
    elif typ == 2:
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        kang = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        for _ in range(kang):
            c = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
            w = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.20, 1.20))
            p += amp * np.exp(-(((x - c) / w) ** 2))
        p += 0.03
    elif typ == 3:
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(1, 4):
            if rng.random() < 0.6:
                amp = rng.uniform(-0.35, 0.35)
                ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
                p += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + ph)
        p += 0.25
    elif typ == 4:
        a = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0))
        b = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0))
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        p += 0.4 * np.sqrt(np.maximum(x, 1e-9)) ** a
        p += 0.4 * np.sqrt(np.maximum(1.0 - x, 1e-9)) ** b
    else:
        pts = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, 6))
        vals = 0.1 + 2.0 * rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, 6)
        p = np.interp(x, pts, vals)
    p = np.clip(p, 1e-9, None)
    return np.log(p)


def choose_hot_count(n):
    if n <= 1024:
        return n
    if n <= 3000:
        return int(max(512, min(n, n // 3)))
    if n <= 10000:
        return int(max(1024, min(n, n // 4)))
    if n <= 20000:
        return int(min(3200, n))
    return int(min(2000, n))


def choose_cd_iters(n):
    if n > 15000:
        return 8
    if n > 8000:
        return 10
    return 12


def choose_active_count(n):
    if n <= 1000:
        return int(min(32, n))
    if n <= 10000:
        return 64
    return 128


def coord_1d_obj(t, base, alpha, center, outside, S_rest, n):
    S = S_rest + t
    if S <= 1e-300:
        return 1e100
    if center < 0:
        m = float(np.max(base + t * alpha))
    else:
        arr = base + t * alpha
        arr[center] = base[center] + t * t
        m = float(np.max(arr))
    tot = m if m > outside else outside
    return float(2.0 * n * tot / (S * S))


def goldensearch_coord(lo, hi, base, alpha, center, outside, S_rest, n, iters):
    if hi <= lo:
        return lo
    gr = 0.6180339887498949
    best_t = lo
    best_f = coord_1d_obj(lo, base, alpha, center, outside, S_rest, n)
    f_hi = coord_1d_obj(hi, base, alpha, center, outside, S_rest, n)
    if f_hi < best_f:
        best_f = f_hi
        best_t = hi

    c = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
    d = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
    fc = coord_1d_obj(c, base, alpha, center, outside, S_rest, n)
    fd = coord_1d_obj(d, base, alpha, center, outside, S_rest, n)
    if fc < best_f:
        best_f, best_t = fc, c
    if fd < best_f:
        best_f, best_t = fd, d

    iters = int(max(0, iters))
    for _ in range(iters):
        if fc < fd:
            hi = d
            d = c
            fd = fc
            c = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
            fc = coord_1d_obj(c, base, alpha, center, outside, S_rest, n)
            if fc < best_f:
                best_f, best_t = fc, c
        else:
            lo = c
            c = d
            fc = fd
            d = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
            fd = coord_1d_obj(d, base, alpha, center, outside, S_rest, n)
            if fd < best_f:
                best_f, best_t = fd, d

    mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    fm = coord_1d_obj(mid, base, alpha, center, outside, S_rest, n)
    if fm < best_f:
        best_f, best_t = fm, mid
    return best_t


def cd_hot_sweep(p, rng, hot_count=None, iters=None, active_count=None):
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return a, np.inf

    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None).copy()
    S = float(a.sum())
    q = fft_conv_self(a)
    M = len(q)

    if hot_count is None:
        hot_count = choose_hot_count(n)
    if iters is None:
        iters = choose_cd_iters(n)
    if active_count is None:
        active_count = choose_active_count(n)
    hot_count = int(max(1, min(n, hot_count)))
    active_count = int(min(len(q), active_count))

    if active_count <= 1:
        idxs = np.array([int(np.argmax(q))], dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idxs = np.argpartition(-q, active_count - 1)[:active_count]

    hot = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k in idxs:
        k = int(k)
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(k, n - 1)
        if hi >= lo:
            w = a[lo:hi + 1] * a[k - lo:k - hi - 1:-1]
            hot[lo:hi + 1] += w

    top_frac = int(0.8 * hot_count)
    top_count = int(min(n, max(0, top_frac)))
    if top_count > 0:
        if top_count >= n:
            top = np.arange(n)
        else:
            top = np.argpartition(-hot, top_count - 1)[:top_count]
        rest = np.setdiff1d(np.arange(n), top, assume_unique=False)
        rand_count = hot_count - top_count
        if rand_count > 0 and len(rest) > 0:
            rand_count = min(rand_count, len(rest))
            rand = rng.choice(rest, size=rand_count, replace=False)
            order = np.concatenate((top, rand))
        else:
            order = top
    else:
        order = np.arange(n)[:hot_count]

    if len(order) > 0 and rng is not None:
        order = rng.permutation(order)

    for ii in order:
        i = int(ii)
        x = float(a[i])
        S_rest = S - x

        k_lo = i
        k_hi = i + n - 1
        if k_hii if hasattr(k_hii, "real") else int(k_hi)]:
        else:
            om_left = 0.0
        if k_hi < M - 1:
            om_right = float(np.max(q[k_hi + 1:]))
        else:
            om_right = 0.0
        outside = max(0.0, om_left, om_right)

        base = q[i:i + n].copy()
        base -= 2.0 * x * a
        base[i] += x * x

        alpha = 2.0 * a.copy()
        alpha[i] = 0.0

        t_upper = max(10.0 * abs(x) + 1e-12, 50.0 / n)
        t_upper = min(1.0, t_upper)

        t_opt = goldensearch_coord(0.0, t_upper, base, alpha, i, outside, S_rest, n, iters)
        if t_opt < 0.0:
            t_opt = 0.0
        t_opt = float(t_opt)

        a[i] = t_opt
        S = S_rest + t_opt

        new_slice = base + t_opt * alpha
        new_slice[i] = base[i] + t_opt * t_opt
        q[i:i + n] = new_slice

    s = float(a.sum())
    if s > 1e-12:
        a = a / s
    return a, score_p(a)


def perturb_p(p, rng, strategy):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = len(p)
    if strategy == 0:
        x = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
        x = x + 0.25 * rng.standard_normal(n)
        out = softmax_vec(x)
        s = float(out.sum())
        return out / s if s > 0 else p
    if strategy == 1:
        out = p.copy()
        out += 0.15 * rng.exponential(1.0 / max(1, n), size=n)
        s = float(out.sum())
        if s > 0:
            return out / s
        return p
    if strategy == 2:
        out = p.copy()
        blen = max(8, n // 80)
        if blen >= n:
            blen = max(1, n // 10)
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - blen + 1))
        saved = float(out[start:start + blen].sum())
        out[start:start + blen] = 0.0
        mask = np.ones(n, dtype=bool)
        mask[start:start + blen] = False
        cnt = int(mask.sum())
        if cnt > 0 and saved > 0:
            out[mask] += saved / cnt
        s = float(out.sum())
        return out / s if s > 0 else p
    if strategy == 3:
        out = p.copy()
        slen = max(8, n // 40)
        s1 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - slen + 1)))
        s2 = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - slen + 1)))
        if s1 != s2:
            tmp = out[s1:s1 + slen].copy()
            out[s1:s1 + slen] = out[s2:s2 + slen]
            out[s2:s2 + slen] = tmp
        s = float(out.sum())
        return out / s if s > 0 else p
    x = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-300))
    x = x + 0.12 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    out = softmax_vec(x)
    s = float(out.sum())
    return out / s if s > 0 else p


def polish_sequence(p, rng, deadline):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        p = np.ones(len(p), dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(len(p))
    p = p / s

    n = len(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = score_p(best_p)
    p_cur = p.copy()

    if n <= 0:
        return best_p, best_score

    # LSE refinement chain.
    qmax = best_score / (2.0 * n) if np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score > 0 else 1.0 / n
    if qmax <= 1e-12:
        qmax = 1.0 / n

    if n <= 1024:
        mfs = [2600, 1800, 1300, 900]
    elif n <= 4096:
        mfs = [2000, 1500, 1100, 800]
    elif n <= 12000:
        mfs = [1500, 1100, 800, 600]
    else:
        mfs = [1100, 800, 600, 450]
    gammas = [1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0]

    for j in range(len(gammas)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 45.0:
            break
        g = gammas[j]
        mf = mfs[j]
        qmax = best_score / (2.0 * n) if np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score > 0 else 1.0 / n
        if qmax <= 1e-12:
            qmax = 1.0 / n
        beta = g / qmax

        sc, p2 = run_lbfgs_from_p(p_cur, beta, mf, mf)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = p2.copy()
                print(f"    LSE gamma={g:g}: {sc:.8f} (NEW BEST)")
            if sc < best_score + 1e-9:
                p_cur = p2

        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
            break
        hc = choose_hot_count(n)
        it = choose_cd_iters(n)
        ac = choose_active_count(n)
        p3, sc3 = cd_hot_sweep(p_cur, rng, hc, it, ac)
        if np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 < best_score:
            best_score = sc3
            best_p = p3.copy()
            p_cur = p3.copy()
            print(f"    CD after LSE: {sc3:.8f} (NEW BEST)")
        elif np.isfinite(sc3) and sc3 < best_score + 1e-9:
            p_cur = p3.copy()

    # Polishing loop with exact coordinate descent and perturbations.
    stale_cd = 0
    stale_pert = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 50.0:
        hc = choose_hot_count(n)
        it = choose_cd_iters(n)
        ac = choose_active_count(n)
        p2, sc2 = cd_hot_sweep(p_cur, rng, hc, it, ac)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc2
            best_p = p2.copy()
            p_cur = p2.copy()
            stale_cd = 0
            stale_pert = 0
            print(f"    CD: {sc2:.8f} (NEW BEST)")
            continue

        stale_cd += 1
        if stale_cd < 3:
            continue

        strat = int(rng.integers(0, 4))
        pp = perturb_p(p_cur, rng, strat)
        qmax = best_score / (2.0 * n) if np.isfinite(best_score) and best_score > 0 else 1.0 / n
        if qmax <= 1e-12:
            qmax = 1.0 / n
        beta = 30.0 / qmax
        scL, pL = run_lbfgs_from_p(pp, beta, 500, 500)
        pCd, scCd = cd_hot_sweep(pL, rng, hc, it, ac)

        cand_sc = scL if scL <= scCd else scCd
        cand_p = pL if scL <= scCd else pCd
        if np.isfinite(cand_sc) and cand_sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cand_sc
            best_p = cand_p.copy()
            p_cur = cand_p.copy()
            stale_cd = 0
            stale_pert = 0
            print(f"    Perturb+LSE+CD: {cand_sc:.8f} (NEW BEST)")
        else:
            stale_pert += 1
            stale_cd = 0
            if stale_pert >= 5:
                break

    return best_p, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    final_deadline = t_start + total_budget - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent_arr = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if parent_arr is None:
        try:
            import sys
            frame = sys._getframe()
            parent_arr = frame.f_globals.get("height_sequence_1", None)
        except Exception:
            parent_arr = None

    if parent_arr is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(parent_arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if arr.size >= 32:
                parent_arr = arr
            else:
                parent_arr = None
        except Exception:
            parent_arr = None

    if parent_arr is None:
        n0 = 8192
        x0 = random_smooth_x(rng, n0, 8.0)
        best_p = softmax_vec(x0)
        print("No parent available; using random smooth initialization.")
    else:
        n0 = len(parent_arr)
        best_p = normalize_nonneg_raw(parent_arr)
        print(f"Parent length: {n0}")

    best_score = score_p(best_p)
    print(f"Initial best: {best_score:.8f}, length={len(best_p)}")

    # Quick multiresolution evaluations.
    lengths = [n0]
    for fac in (0.5, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0):
        nl = int(round(n0 * fac))
        if 512 <= nl <= 30000 and nl not in lengths:
            lengths.append(nl)
    npow = 1
    while npow < n0:
        npow <<= 1
    if 512 <= npow <= 30000 and npow not in lengths:
        lengths.append(npow)

    parent_for_resample = best_p.copy()
    for nl in lengths:
        p_try = resample_p(parent_for_resample, nl)
        sc = score_p(p_try)
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p_try.copy()
            print(f"  Resampled to n={nl}: {sc:.8f} (NEW BEST)")

    # A cheap exact coordinate-descent polish on current best.
    if time.monotonic() < t_start + 180.0 and len(best_p) <= 25000:
        p_cd, sc_cd = cd_hot_sweep(
            best_p,
            rng,
            choose_hot_count(len(best_p)),
            choose_cd_iters(len(best_p)),
            choose_active_count(len(best_p)),
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc_cd) and sc_cd < best_score:
            best_score = sc_cd
            best_p = p_cd.copy()
            print(f"  Initial CD polish: {sc_cd:.8f} (NEW BEST)")

    # Coarse global search.
    nc = max(256, min(1024, max(256, n0 // 8)))
    nc_pow = 1
    while nc_pow < nc:
        nc_pow <<= 1
    if 256 <= nc_pow <= 1024:
        nc = nc_pow

    coarse_deadline = min(final_deadline, t_start + 0.30 * total_budget, t_start + 780.0)
    coarse_p0 = resample_p(best_p, nc)
    coarse_score = score_p(coarse_p0)
    coarse_best_p = coarse_p0.copy()
    print(f"Coarse global search: n={nc}, initial coarse score={coarse_score:.8f}")

    coarse_mfs = [300, 500, 700]
    coarse_gammas = [1.0, 5.0, 20.0]
    restart = 0
    while time.monotonic() < coarse_deadline:
        restart += 1
        if restart % 20 == 0:
            qs = coarse_score / (2.0 * nc) if coarse_score > 0 else 1.0 / nc
            print(f"  coarse restart {restart}, best coarse score={coarse_score:.8f}")

        if restart == 1:
            x_init = np.log(np.maximum(t_start + 0.08 * total_budget
    while time.monotonic() < p_deadline and len(initial_lengths) < 512:
        pass
    if time.monotonic() < p_deadline:
        print(f"Initial multires table (n={len(initial_lengths)}): best={init_pool_best:.8f}")

    # Coarse global search.
    nc = max(256, min(1024, n0 // 10))
    nc_pow = 1
    while nc_pow < nc:
        nc_pow <<= 1
    if 256 <= nc_pow <= 1024:
        nc = nc_pow

    coarse_deadline = min(quick_deadline, t_start + 0.28 * total_budget, t_start + 700.0)
    coarse_p0 = resample_pool_p(initial_pool_best_p, nc)
    coarse_score = score_p(coarse_p0)
    coarse_best_p = coarse_p0.copy()
    print(f"Coarse search length={nc}, initial={coarse_score:.8f}")

    coarse_gammas = [1.0, 4.0, 12.0, 40.0]
    coarse_mfs = [300, 500, 700, 900]
    restart = 0
    while time.monotonic() < coarse_deadline:
        restart += 1
        if restart % 10 == 0:
            print(f"  coarse restart {restart}, best coarse={coarse_score:.8f}, global={best_score:.8f}")

        if restart % 4 == 0:
            x_init = np.log(np.maximum(coarse_best_p, 1e-300))
            x_init = x_init + (0.8 + 0.6 * rng.random()) * rng.standard_normal(nc)
        elif restart % 4 == 1:
            x_init = random_smooth_x(rng, nc, int(4 + 8 * rng.random()))
        elif restart % 4 == 2:
            x_init = param_x(rng, nc)
        else:
            x_init = np.log(np.maximum(coarse_best_p, 1e-300))
            x_init = x_init + 0.35 * rng.standard_normal(nc)

        p_init = softmax_vec(x_init)
        sc_init = score_p(p_init)
        if sc_init < coarse_score:
            coarse_score = sc_init
            coarse_best_p = p_init.copy()

        qmax = sc_init / (2.0 * nc) if sc_init > 0 else 1.0 / nc
        if qmax <= 1e-12:
            qmax = 1.0 / nc

        p_stage = p_init
        sc_stage = sc_init
        for gi in range(len(coarse_gammas)):
            if time.monotonic() >= coarse_deadline:
                break
            beta = coarse_gammas[gi] / qmax
            mf = coarse_mfs[gi]
            sc_next, p_next = run_lbfgs_from_p(p_stage, beta, mf, mf)
            if np.isfinite(sc_next):
                p_stage = p_next
                sc_stage = sc_next
                if sc_stage < coarse_score:
                    coarse_score = sc_stage
                    coarse_best_p = p_stage.copy()
                    # occasionally promote to full-length seeds if promising
                    if coarse_score < best_score * 1.01 and len(initial_lengths) < 9:
                        p_full = resample_pool_p(p_stage, len(best_p))
                        sc_full = score_p(p_full)
                        if sc_full < best_score * 1.02:
                            initial_pool_best = min(initial_pool_best, sc_full)
                            initial_pool_best_p = p_full.copy()
                            initial_lengths.append(len(p_full))
                            print(f"  Coarse candidate promoted to n={len(p_full)}: {sc_full:.8f}")

        # CD polish on the coarse level for the best coarse point.
        if time.monotonic() < coarse_deadline - 5.0:
            p_cd, sc_cd = cd_hot_sweep(coarse_best_p, rng, choose_hot_count(nc), choose_cd_iters(nc), choose_active_count(nc))
            if np.isfinite(sc_cd) and sc_cd < coarse_score:
                coarse_score = sc_cd
                coarse_best_p = p_cd.copy()

    # Upgrade the best coarse sequence to several target lengths.
    if coarse_best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 120.0:
        for nl in target_lengths:
            if time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 120.0:
                break
            p_try = resample_pool_p(coarse_best_p, nl)
            sc = score_p(p_try)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                initial_pool_best = min(initial_pool_best, sc)
                initial_pool_best_p = p_try.copy()
                initial_lengths.append(nl)

    # A final exact coordinate polish on a few promising lengths.
    refined_p = bresample_pool_p(initial_pool_best_p, seed_len)
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 90.0:
        refined_p, refined_score = cd_hot_sweep(
            refined_p,
            rng,
            choose_hot_count(len(refined_p)),
            choose_cd_iters(len(refined_p)),
            choose_active_count(len(refined_p)),
        )
        if np.isfinite(refined_score) and refined_score < best_score:
            best_score = refined_score
            best_p = refined_p.copy()
            print(f"Pre-refinement CD: {refined_score:.8f}")

    # Main refinement pass on the current global best.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 120.0:
        best_p, best_score = polish_sequence(best_p, rng, final_deadline - 60.0)

    # If the program has time and the coarse branch is distinct, refine it too.
    if coarse_best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 120.0:
        cand_len = len(best_p)
        p_try = resample_pool_p(coarse_best_p, cand_len)
        sc_try = score_p(p_try)
        if sc_try < best_score * 1.02:
            p_try, sc_pol = polish_sequence(p_try, rng, min(final_deadline - 60.0, time.monotonic() + 0.35 * total_budget))
            if sc_pol < best_score:
                best_score = sc_pol
                best_p = p_try.copy()
                print(f"Secondary coarse refinement improved to {sc_pol:.8f}")

    # Very late small polish, if possible.
    if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 35.0:
        p_late = best_p.copy()
        p_late, sc_late = cd_hot_sweep(
            p_late,
            rng,
            choose_hot_count(len(p_late)),
            choose_cd_iters(len(p_late)),
            choose_active_count(len(p_late)),
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc_late) and sc_late < best_score:
            best_score = sc_late
            best_p = p_late.copy()

    out_raw = np.clip(np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64).ravel(), 0.0, 1000.0)
    s_out = float(out_raw.sum())
    if s_out < 0.01:
        out_raw = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in out_raw]
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = np.inf

    if not np.isfinite(verified) or verified >= 1e100:
        fallback = np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        fallback = np.clip(fallback, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if fallback.sum() < 0.01:
            fallback = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) * 0.01
        out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in fallback]

    print("=" * 64)
    print(f"FINAL INTERNAL = {best_score:.8f}")
    try:
        print(f"FINAL VERIFIED = {float(evaluate_sequence(out)):.8f}")
    except Exception as ee:
        print("Verification call failed:", ee)
    print(f"Length = {len(out)}, time = {time.monotonic() - t_start:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 64)

    return out
```
